Only an open or read failure of the file is operational (exit 1). Recorded in DECISIONS.json.\n\n## Purpose\n\nProvide the single entry point through which every report reads Drydock's LLM execution log. The reader yields each non-blank line as raw bytes in file order. Parsing, skipping, and aggregation happen downstream.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The file path is opened and read as UTF-8 bytes, one line at a time.\n- Lines that are empty or contain only whitespace are ignored and are not counted as skipped.\n- Each non-blank line is passed on as raw bytes, with its one-based line number preserved for diagnostics.\n- A file that cannot be opened or read returns an operational error to the caller, which maps it to exit 1.\n- The reader never writes to the log file.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC log-read-blank-lines ===\nIntent: Blank lines are ignored by the line reader and do not appear in the yielded lines.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ncontent = '{\"execution_id\":\"a\"}\\n\\n   \\n{\"execution_id\":\"b\"}\\n'\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"log.jsonl\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fh:\n        fh.write(content)\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"summary\", path)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0, (code, out, err)\n    rows = out.splitlines()\n    assert rows[0].startswith(\"records,skipped,\"), out\n    fields = rows[1].split(\",\")\n    assert fields[0] == \"2\", out\n    assert fields[1] == \"0\", out\n=== END AC log-read-blank-lines ===\n\n=== AC log-read-utf8 ===\nIntent: The line reader reads the file as UTF-8 and a multibyte character inside a valid record is kept intact.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; a line skipped for a missing field does not block a later valid line with the same id. The sample passes under either reading. Recorded in DECISIONS.json.\n\n## Purpose\n\nTurn raw log lines into kept records and a whole-file skipped count. Malformed and duplicate lines are never fatal; they are skipped, counted, and reported on stderr.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Each non-blank line is parsed as a JSON object. A line that is not a JSON object is skipped.\n- A line is skipped when any of `execution_id`, `started_at`, `status`, `job.command_name`, or `job.target` is absent or not a non-empty string.\n- A line whose `execution_id` already appeared on an earlier kept record is skipped. The first occurrence is kept.\n- Each skipped line writes exactly one warning to standard error naming its one-based line number. Nothing is written to standard output.\n- Skipping never changes the exit code.\n- The skipped count covers the whole file and is independent of any filter.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC zero, never a skip reason (Rule 3).\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-LogFailed.md ===\n# Feature: Log Failed\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Classifies a kept record as failed when its status is any value other than succeeded, and exposes the fields the failures report needs. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-LogSkip.md |\n| Provides    | Record.Failed, nullable returncode, timed_out |\n| Consumes    | record parser |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nIdentify failed calls for the summary, command, target, model, daily, and failures reports.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- A record is failed when `status` is any value other than `succeeded`.\n- The record exposes `started_at`, `job.target`, `job.command_name`, its model (per FEATURE-LogModel), `result.returncode` as nullable, and `result.timed_out` for the failures report.\n- `returncode` is absent or `null` when the record |\n\n## Questions\n\n- Q-005 (low, system_choice `skip-line`): a line whose bytes are not valid UTF-8 cannot parse as a JSON object, so it is skipped under the malformed-line rule rather than failing the run. Only an open or read failure of the file is operational (exit 1). Recorded in DECISIONS.json.\n\n## Purpose\n\nProvide the single entry point through which every report reads Drydock's LLM execution log. The reader yields each non-blank line as raw bytes in file order. Parsing, skipping, and aggregation happen downstream.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The file path is opened and read as UTF-8 bytes, one line at a time.\n- Lines that are empty or contain only whitespace are ignored and are not counted as skipped.\n- Each non-blank line is passed on as raw bytes, with its one-based line number preserved for diagnostics.\n- A file that cannot be opened or read returns an operational error to the caller, which maps it to exit 1.\n- The reader never writes to the log file.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC log-read-blank-lines ===\nIntent: Blank lines are ignored by the line reader and do not appear in the yielded lines.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; The sample passes under either reading. Recorded in DECISIONS.json.\n\n## Purpose\n\nTurn raw log lines into kept records and a whole-file skipped count. Malformed and duplicate lines are never fatal; they are skipped, counted, and reported on stderr.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Each non-blank line is parsed as a JSON object. The first occurrence is kept.\n- Each skipped line writes exactly one warning to standard error naming its one-based line number. Nothing is written to standard output.\n- Skipping never changes the exit code.\n- The skipped count covers the whole file and is independent of any filter.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC zero, never a skip reason (Rule 3).\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-LogFailed.md ===\n# Feature: Log Failed\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Classifies a kept record as failed when its status is any value other than succeeded, and exposes the fields the failures report needs. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-LogSkip.md |\n| Provides    | Record.Failed, nullable returncode, timed_out |\n| Consumes    | record parser |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nIdentify failed calls for the summary, command, target, model, daily, and failures reports.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- A record is failed when `status` is any value other than `succeeded`.\n- The record exposes `started_at`, `job.target`, `job.command_name`, its model (per FEATURE-LogModel), `result.returncode` as nullable, and `result.timed_out` for the failures report.\n- `returncode` is absent or `null` when the record has no return code; `timed_out` is treated as false when absent.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC log-failed-status-classification ===\nIntent: A record whose status is not succeeded is counted as failed in the summary, and a succeeded record is not.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nok =
